Wording

English
Let $x_{2}$ be a point of $J$. Let $F$ be a functor of $C$ and $D$. Let $K$ be a Grothendieck topology of $D$. Let $P$ be a functor of the set of opposites of $D$ and $A$. Let $X$ be an element of $C$. Let $x_{4}$ be an element of the action of the fiber of $x_{2}$ and $X$. Then the presheaf fiber map of $X$ and $x_{4}$ is a homomorphism of the action of $P$ and $\operatorname{op}$ applied to the action of $F$ and $X$ and the action of the presheaf fiber of the map of $F$ and $K$ and $P$ defined as the presheaf fiber of the element of $X$ and $x_{4}$ and $P$.

French

Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Sites.Point.OfIsCofiltered

Wording
no wording
English
Let $p$ be a functor of $N$ and $C$ such that for all elements $X$ of $C$, for all sieves $R$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoT {J (X)}{R}$, then for all elements $U$ of $N$, for all homomorphisms $f$ of the action of $p$ and $U$ and $X$, there exists an element $Y$ of $C$, such that there exists a homomorphism $g$ of $Y$ and $X$, such that there exists an element $h_{5}$ of the arrow of $R$, such that there exists an element $V$ of $N$, such that there exists a homomorphism $q$ of $V$ and $U$, such that there exists a homomorphism $a$ of the action of $p$ and $V$ and $Y$, such that the composition of $a$ and $g$ is equal to the composition of the map of $p$ and $q$ and $f$. Let $P$ be a functor of the set of opposites of $C$ and $A$. Then $\operatorname{isColimitPresheafFiberOfIsCofilteredCocone}$ applied to $p$, $\mathrm{hp}$ and $P$ is an element of $\operatorname{IsColimit}$ applied to the presheaf fiber of $p$ and $P$ defined as the inverse function of $\operatorname{isColimitWhiskerEquiv}$ applied to the opposite of the functor of $p$ and the cocone of the action of the action of the left whiskering of the set of opposites of $C$ and $A$ and the opposite of the projection of the fiber of the point of $p$ and $\mathrm{hp}$ and $P$ and the cocone of the point of $p$ and $\mathrm{hp}$ and $P$.
Formula

French

Wording
the point of p and hp (from the name)
English
Let $p$ be a functor of $N$ and $C$ such that for all elements $X$ of $C$, for all sieves $R$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoT {J (X)}{R}$, then for all elements $U$ of $N$, for all homomorphisms $f$ of the action of $p$ and $U$ and $X$, there exists an element $Y$ of $C$, such that there exists a homomorphism $g$ of $Y$ and $X$, such that there exists an element $h_{4}$ of the arrow of $R$, such that there exists an element $V$ of $N$, such that there exists a homomorphism $q$ of $V$ and $U$, such that there exists a homomorphism $a$ of the action of $p$ and $V$ and $Y$, such that the composition of $a$ and $g$ is equal to the composition of the map of $p$ and $q$ and $f$. Then the point of $p$ and $\mathrm{hp}$ is a point of $J$.
Formula

CategoryTheory.GrothendieckTopology.Point.ofIsCofiltered.fiberdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Sites.Point.OfIsCofiltered, function or constant
Wording
the fiber of p (from the name)
English
Let $p$ be a functor of $N$ and $C$. Then the fiber of $p$ is a functor of $C$ and the set of sets defined as the composition of the Yoneda embedding and the composition of the action of the left whiskering of the set of opposites of $C$ and the set of sets and the opposite of $p$ and the colimit.
Formula
$p \in \operatorname{Functor} (N , C)\vdash \operatorname{fiber} (p):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{shrinkYoneda} , \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{whiskeringLeft} (\mathrm{Opposite} (N), \mathrm{Opposite} (C), \mathrm{Set}), \operatorname{op} (p)), \operatorname{colim}))\in \operatorname{Functor} (C , \mathrm{Set})$
French
Soit $p$ un foncteur de $N$ et $C$. Alors la fibre de $p$ est un foncteur de $C$ et l'ensemble d'ensembles défini comme la composition du plongement d'Yonedas et la composition de l'action du whiskering gauche de l'ensemble d'opposés de $C$ et l'ensemble d'ensembles et l'opposé de $p$ et la colimite.

Then the amalgamation of $i_{2}$ and $\mathrm{hf}$ is a homomorphism of $E$ and the action of $P$ and $\operatorname{op}$ applied to $S$ defined as the function that maps $x_{2}$ and $\mathrm{hx}$ to $\operatorname{choose}$ applied to $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Presheaf\_\_IsSheaf\_\_existsUnique\_amalgamation\_ofArrows}$ applied to $\mathrm{hP}$, $i_{2}$, $f$, $\mathrm{hf}$ and $i_{1}$, $x_{2}$ and $\mathrm{hx}$.

CategoryTheory.Presieve.IsSeparateddefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Sites.SheafOfTypes, predicate
Wording
J is separated (from the docstring)
English
Let $J$ be a Grothendieck topology of $C$. Let $P$ be a functor of the set of opposites of $C$ and the set of sets. Then $J$ is separated, if for all elements $X$ of $C$, for all sieves $S$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoT {J (X)}{S}$, then $P$ is separated.
Formula
$J \in \operatorname{GrothendieckTopology} (C), P \in \operatorname{Functor} (\mathrm{Opposite} (C), \mathrm{Set})\vdash \operatorname{IsSeparated} (J , P):\Leftrightarrow \forall X \in C . \forall S \in \operatorname{Sieve} (X). \MembershipmemMACRoAA {J (X)}{S}\rightarrow \operatorname{IsSeparatedFor} (P , \operatorname{arrows} (S))$
French

presieve of X (from the name)
English
Let $X$ be an element of $C$. Then a presieve of $X$ is an element of the function that maps $X$ to the set of functions from elements $Y$ of $C$ and homomorphisms of $Y$ and $X$ to propositions.
Formula

Let $h$ be a small category of set of $x$ and $C$. Then the arrow equivalence of $h$ is an equivalence from the set of arrows of the set of elements of the object of the small category of $h$ to the set of arrows of $C$.
Formula
$h \in \operatorname{CoreSmallCategoryOfSet} (x , C)\vdash \operatorname{arrowEquiv} (h)\in \EquivMACRoAL {\operatorname{Arrow} (\operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{smallCategoryOfSet} (h))))}{\operatorname{Arrow} (C)}$
French
Soit $h$ une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$ et $C$. Alors l'équivalence de flèches de $h$ est une équivalence de l'ensemble de flèches de l'ensemble d'éléments de l'objet de la catégorie petite de $h$ à l'ensemble de flèches de $C$.
CategoryTheory.CoreSmallCategoryOfSet.equivalencedefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, function or constant
Wording
the equivalence of h (from the name)
English
Let $h$ be a small category of set of $x$ and $C$. Then the equivalence of $h$ is an equivalence of the set of elements of the object of the small category of $h$ and $C$ defined as $\operatorname{asEquivalence}$ applied to the functor of $h$.
Formula
$h \in \operatorname{CoreSmallCategoryOfSet} (x , C)\vdash \operatorname{equivalence} (h):= \operatorname{asEquivalence} (\operatorname{functor} (h))\in \operatorname{Equivalence} (\operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{smallCategoryOfSet} (h))), C)$
French
Soit $h$ une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$ et $C$. Alors l'équivalence de $h$ est une équivalence de l'ensemble d'éléments de l'objet de la catégorie petite de $h$ et $C$ défini comme $\operatorname{asEquivalence}$ appliqué à le foncteur de $h$.

CategoryTheory.CoreSmallCategoryOfSet.homdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, function or constant
Wording
the homomorphism of X and Y (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a small category of set of $x$ and $C$. Let $X$ and $Y$ be elements of the object of $\mathrm{self}$. Then the homomorphism of $X$ and $Y$ is an element of $\wp x$ defined as $\operatorname{proj1}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$, $X$ and $Y$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{CoreSmallCategoryOfSet} (x , C), X , Y \in \operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{obj} (\mathrm{self}))\vdash \operatorname{hom} (\mathrm{self} , X , Y):= \operatorname{proj1} (\mathrm{self} , X , Y)\in \wp x$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$ et $C$. Soient $X$ et $Y$ des éléments de l'objet de $\mathrm{self}$. Alors l'homomorphisme de $X$ et $Y$ est un élément de $\wp x$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj1}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$, $X$ et $Y$.
CategoryTheory.CoreSmallCategoryOfSet.homEquivdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, function or constant
Wording
the homomorphism equivalence of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a small category of set of $x$ and $C$. Then the homomorphism equivalence of $\mathrm{self}$ is an equivalence from the set of elements of the homomorphism of $X$ and $Y$ to the set of homomorphisms of $\operatorname{objEquiv}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $X$ and $\operatorname{objEquiv}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $Y$ defined as $\operatorname{proj3}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{CoreSmallCategoryOfSet} (x , C)\vdash \operatorname{homEquiv} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{proj3} (\mathrm{self})\in \EquivMACRoAL {\operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{hom} (\mathrm{self} , X , Y))}{\operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{objEquiv} (\mathrm{self} , X), \operatorname{objEquiv} (\mathrm{self} , Y))}$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$ et $C$. Alors l'équivalence d'homomorphismes de $\mathrm{self}$ est une équivalence de l'ensemble d'éléments de l'homomorphisme de $X$ et $Y$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de $\operatorname{objEquiv}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $X$ et $\operatorname{objEquiv}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $Y$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj3}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

Wording
the object of self (from the name)

Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a small category of set of $x$ and $C$. Then the object of $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of $\wp x$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.

CategoryTheory.CoreSmallCategoryOfSet.smallCategoryOfSetdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, function or constant
Wording
the small category of h (from the name)
English
Let $h$ be a small category of set of $x$ and $C$. Then the small category of $h$ is a small category of set of $x$.
Formula
$h \in \operatorname{CoreSmallCategoryOfSet} (x , C)\vdash \operatorname{smallCategoryOfSet} (h)\in \operatorname{SmallCategoryOfSet} (x)$
French
Soit $h$ une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$ et $C$. Alors la catégorie petite de $h$ est une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$.

CategoryTheory.SmallCategoryCardinalLT.categoryFamilydefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, type
Wording
category family of x and S (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a cardinal. Let $S$ be an element of $\operatorname{SmallCategoryCardinalLT}$ applied to $x$. Then a category family of $x$ and $S$ is an element of the function that maps $x$ and $S$ to the set of elements of the object of $S$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Cardinal} , S \in \operatorname{SmallCategoryCardinalLT} (x)\vdash \operatorname{categoryFamily} (x , S):= \lambda x , S . \operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{obj} (S))$
French
Soit $x$ un cardinal. Soit $S$ un élément de $\operatorname{SmallCategoryCardinalLT}$ appliqué à $x$. Alors une famille de catégories de $x$ et $S$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $x$ et $S$ à l'ensemble d'éléments de l'objet de $S$.
CategoryTheory.SmallCategoryOfSetstructureMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, type
Wording
small category of set of x (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then the small categories of set of $x$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{SmallCategoryOfSet} (x)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors les catégories d'ensemble petites de $x$ sont un type de base.
CategoryTheory.SmallCategoryOfSet.categoryFamilydefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, type
Wording
category family of x and x1 (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Let $x_{1}$ be a small category of set of $x$. Then a category family of $x$ and $x_{1}$ is an element of the function that maps $x$ and $x_{1}$ to the set of elements of the object of $x_{1}$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} , x_{1} \in \operatorname{SmallCategoryOfSet} (x)\vdash \operatorname{categoryFamily} (x , x_{1}):= \lambda x , x_{1} . \operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{obj} (x_{1}))$

CategoryTheory.SmallCategoryOfSet.compdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.SmallRepresentatives, function or constant
Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a small category of set of $x$. Let $f$ be an element of the homomorphism of $X$ and $Y$. Let $g$ be an element of the homomorphism of $Y$ and $Z$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is an element of the homomorphism of $X$ and $Z$ defined as $\operatorname{proj3}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$, $f$ and $g$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{SmallCategoryOfSet} (x), f \in \operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{hom} (\mathrm{self} , X , Y)), g \in \operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{hom} (\mathrm{self} , Y , Z))\vdash \operatorname{comp} (\mathrm{self} , f , g):= \operatorname{proj3} (\mathrm{self} , f , g)\in \operatorname{Elem} (\operatorname{hom} (\mathrm{self} , X , Z))$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ une catégorie d'ensemble petite de $x$. Soit $f$ un élément de l'homomorphisme de $X$ et $Y$. Soit $g$ un élément de l'homomorphisme de $Y$ et $Z$. Alors la composition de $f$ et $g$ est un élément de l'homomorphisme de $X$ et $Z$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj3}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$, $f$ et $g$.

Wording
the identity of self and X (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a small category of set of $x$. Let $X$ be an element of the object of $\mathrm{self}$. Then the identity of $\mathrm{self}$ and $X$ is an element of the homomorphism of $X$ and $X$ defined as $\operatorname{proj2}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $X$.
